# Harness / system results ledger (append-only; one line per landed S-row result)

Format: date | area | gap | metric before -> after (n, denominator) | verdict | commit
Same rules as docs/evidence/tracking/RESULTS_LEDGER.md: only measured numbers with an artifact; honest REJECT / NULL / BEHIND / FALSIFIED / CLOSED AT LIMIT lines belong here. Calibration language only (Q6): no dollar, ROI, profit or edge word. Program-day label 2026-09-03 = the planning set date; hook-written lines carry the local clock date.

ESS gate runs FIRST: rho is estimated from the MONITORED window's own rows via gap_effective_n.intraclass_correlation (never a stored constant) and n_eff < 30 makes every monitor INSUFFICIENT, so a thin window cannot alarm. (a) calibration decay composes ledger.drift_report(rows, now_iso, 7.0, 30.0, k_sigma=1.0) UNTOUCHED, recovering its own SE as (threshold - baseline_brier)/k_sigma and re-inflating it by sqrt(design_effect); (b) crowding: trailing-30-day mean |prob - p_close| < 0.5 x the first-30-day mean; (c) regime drift: scipy chi-square on regime_calibration.buckets shares, monitored vs fitting window, p < 0.05, expected >= 5 per cell with smaller cells merged into OTHER. ALL THREE ARE REPORT-ONLY -- none disables anything, and write_report's out_path defaults to None so nothing was written to docs/evidence/calibration/ today. `python -m pytest scripts/platformkit/eval_gate/test_decay_monitors.py -q` = 3 passed in 4.78s: thin (9 games x 10 rows, loss constant within game -> rho 1.0) n_eff 9.0 all INSUFFICIENT; drifted crowding 0.02 against a 0.05 threshold ALARM and regime p 2.35e-61 ALARM; stable regime p 1.0, crowding 0.08, recent Brier 0.111053 under a widened threshold 0.116723, all OK. AFTER: ValueError in both modes, both inputs. RT-19 clv_ledger_io.py:130-136 `ledger_lock` was documented never to raise: MEASURED on this box (_LOCK_KIND="msvcrt", a real backend) a contended _acquire returned False after 5.02s and the critical section RAN UNLOCKED 5.01s later -- two charges can both read prior=K and both write K+1, a lost update that UNDERCOUNTS K. AFTER: LedgerLockError, body never runs. THE LINEUP came from events instead, which is what the row asked for: data/cache/team_system/pbp*/<nba_game_id>.json (3,652 games; ~100-150 substitution actions per game carrying personId + subType in/out) -- all 1,280 bridged priced games have a pbp file. lineup_at() applies ONLY events with event_elapsed < tick_elapsed and assert_strictly_before raises AsOfViolation on a same-tick or later read (both tested); starters are inferred (first appearance is a non-sub action, or a sub the player is leaving on). Nothing falsified. (a) NEW scripts/platformkit/ops/safe_parquet_write.py -- write_parquet_atomic() writes to a mkstemp sibling and os.replace()s it (bounded Windows PermissionError backoff, temp unlinked and target untouched on any failure), and REFUSES to replace an existing parquet with fewer rows (ShrinkRefused, logged) unless allow_shrink=True; the existing count is read from the parquet FOOTER and a failed read PROPAGATES, so an unreadable file is never silently overwritten. Wired into all 19 sites: the log.warning('-- overwriting') became `raise RuntimeError(...) from exc` and every to_parquet became write_parquet_atomic. Refusing to shrink is safe for exactly these writers because each concats existing+new and dedups keep='last', so the merged frame is a superset of what is on disk. Tests 6 passed (grow, equal-count re-run, shrink refused with the 20-row file still reading 20, explicit allow_shrink, garbage existing file raises and its bytes are unchanged, failed write leaves no partial and no stray temp); every pre-existing ingest test re-run green in master (kbo+npb 26, wnba 21, mlb 29, soccer 19). 2026-09-03 | signals-ingame | S101 | ADAPTIVE CONFORMAL COVERAGE ON NBA TICKS -- THE INTERVAL PROBLEM S97 LEFT OPEN IS FIXABLE, BUT NOT TO THE BAR. PREMISE CONFIRMED (Q8), not falsified: scripts/platformkit/ingame/aci_online.py has exactly one production caller and it is nba-hardcoded (aci_stream_shim.py:15-16,109 imported from ingame_pred_tick_runner.py:145-146 apply_to_document(doc,'nba') and :200-201 update_stream('nba')), data/cache/ingame_aci DOES NOT EXIST, and no artifact under data/cache/ or docs/evidence/ had ever scored it on ticks. Its public API takes NO nonconformity score -- it takes a base interval per tick plus the realised y, forms err_t = 1[y_t not in [lo_t,hi_t]], updates a_next = clip(a_t + gamma*(alpha_target - err_t), 0, 1) (aci_update:25, err_t in {0,1} enforced by ValueError:34) and rescales the half-width by min(alpha_target/max(alpha_t,1e-6), 100) (apply_aci_to_band:51); _MIN_STREAM_LEN=50. The L17 shim blocker is unchanged and was not touched (ingame_grade rows still carry no lo/hi); S101 calls run_aci_stream directly as L17 directs. CONSTRUCTION: a per-tick score |y - p| is degenerate (y in {0,1} is never inside a 0.02-wide probability band), so the calibration is GROUPED on both sides of the pipe -- within each phase cell, TRAIN-fold ticks are cut into equal-count groups (>=400, cap 50) ordered by p, a group's score is |mean(p) - realised group frequency|, and the cell half-width is the (1-alpha) empirical quantile (method=higher); cells too small for 2 groups inherit the pooled quantile (12 cells on fold 1 rising to 20 on fold 5; pooled half-width 0.0766-0.1334). Two point predictors, the raw in-play market and the S86 as-of prior; two arms, STATIC (train-calibrated band held fixed -- the leak-free deliverable) and ACI (run_aci_stream walked per held-out GAME in ts order, alpha reset at every boundary, gamma at aci_online's own default 0.01, 0 games INSUFFICIENT_DATA). Expanding walk-forward by game-first date, 5 folds, purge asserted game-disjoint and a 1-day embargo asserted per fold, fold windows byte-identical to S94's and S97's so S101 and S97 are scored on the SAME ROWS: 192,635 ticks / 673 games scored, 40,316-tick seed never scored. HEADLINE at nominal 0.90, grouped coverage in S97's own definition: the raw market's conformal band reaches P1 0.9362 P2 0.9400 P3 0.9600 P4 0.9800 OT 0.9412 ALL 0.9600 against S97's Gaussian posterior at P1 0.191 P2 0.120 P3 0.220 P4 0.080 OT 0.000 ALL 0.080 -- the deficit SIGN FLIPS from 68-90 points of under-coverage to 2-8 points of OVER-coverage, at 4-11x the width (P1 0.0221 -> 0.2336). The comparison that carries the mechanism is against S97's own in-sample inflation diagnostic, which found P1 needs k=15 (width 0.3152, coverage 0.936) and that P3, P4, OT and ALL reach 0.90 at NO scalar inflation up to k=200: S101 hits 0.936 at P1 at width 0.2336 -- narrower than S97's in-sample answer and out of sample -- and reaches 0.94-0.98 at P3/P4/OT where no Gaussian width could, because a conformal half-width fitted per cell to the actual |p - frequency| deviation absorbs the LOCATION bias S97 named and a symmetric variance inflation cannot. BAR 90 +/- 2 PER PHASE: NOT REACHED, 0 of 5 phases for either predictor; the single closest is the as-of prior in P2 at 0.8800 whose deviation is -0.020000000000000018 in float, recorded as a MISS and the tolerance NOT widened. The as-of prior in OT is the one hard failure, 0.6471 at width 0.3760 against the market band's 0.9412 at 0.0562 -- the OT repricer artifact S98 measured on Brier, reproduced on coverage. At nominal 0.80 the bar is also missed 0 of 5 and the over-coverage is WORSE than at 0.90 (market ALL +0.16 vs +0.06): lowering the target shrinks the half-width far less than the nominal drop requires because the calibration quantile is taken over 2-50 cell groups whose upper tail is thin -- a real limit of the grouped calibration, not a tuning artifact. THE ONLINE ACI ARM SATURATES and is reported as a LABEL-CONSUMING DIAGNOSTIC, never as a leak-free result: coverage 1.000 in essentially every phase at 5x the static width (ALL 0.5160 vs 0.0959; 1.22 wide in P2, the whole probability line), which is the trivial solution. Mechanically, early in a game p sits near 0.5 and the band is ~0.09 wide so EVERY tick is a miss, alpha falls at 0.9*gamma per tick and the band widens on no evidence; late in the game p converges to y, misses stop and alpha climbs back. ALPHA TRAJECTORY (market, 0.90): mean alpha used 0.0592 against a 0.10 target, mean alpha over each game's last quarter 0.0916, ticks pinned at the zero clip 0.73 pct, per-game terminal alpha 0.0905-0.0954 across folds with tail std 0.0044-0.0066, and 16-46 pct of games touch the zero clip at some point -- so alpha SETTLES, but per game after a down-and-back excursion rather than to a stable global level, and for the as-of prior it settles ABOVE target (0.90: terminal 0.167-0.201; 0.80: terminal 0.357-0.413), i.e. the update tries to NARROW the prior's band even though the prior's coverage is the one that fails. WHY IT IS LABEL-CONSUMING, measured: y has nunique == 1 for all 797 games, one label attached to every tick, so ANY within-game online update reads that game's own final outcome however the strictly-before guard is written -- the guard is real and tested (no tick's interval depends on a later tick) but the quantity it reads at earlier ticks is still the result. A leak-free online form would update alpha between COMPLETED games; that is a different row and was not built. gate_aci_on_stream's planted null returns null_collapses False / REJECT:planted_null on all four (arm, nominal) pairs and is recorded NOT INFORMATIVE rather than as a verdict, because its internal criteria are the per-tick binary coverage and pinball loss -- the degenerate form S101 exists to replace. NAMED NEXT STEPS, neither taken: a finer conformal grid on the calibration side only with the scoring resolution left at S97's 400 so the bar is unmoved, and an asymmetric band, since every under-covering cell here is a location miss that p +/- hw cannot express. LIMITS: the coverage measure cannot resolve the bar in OT (17 groups -> resolution 0.0588, coarser than +/- 0.02; the bar is exactly one group wide everywhere, inherited from S97 and not changed because changing it moves the bar); gamma is aci_online's default not a fitted value and no sweep was run (a different gamma moves the ACI width but cannot fix a saturation that is a property of the binary error indicator); the conformal quantile has no finite-sample correction and method=higher is conservative in the same direction as the observed over-coverage, so part of the +2 to +8 point excess is that and the two are not separated. Landed: scripts/platformkit/eval_gate/s101_aci_coverage.py (276 LOC) + tests/platformkit/ingame/test_s101_aci_coverage.py (8 passed -- the strictly-before guard proved by rewriting every outcome at/after tick 90 and asserting alpha and the bands at ticks 0..90 are bit-identical WHILE the ticks after it do move; per-game alpha reset; INSUFFICIENT_DATA falling back to the static band; calibration determinism and quantile monotonicity in alpha; grouped coverage exercised at both extremes and below the 2-group floor; and the three S97 bars asserted byte-identical plus an ASCII/no-edge-language scan of the module source). STATE STALENESS WAS THE DOMINANT DEFECT: the joined series stops when the capture stops but the price series runs to settlement, so a plain backward as-of join carries a two-hour-stale score forward -- mlb moneyline Brier 0.3234 vs market 0.2130 before a 300 s tolerance, 0.2010 vs 0.1606 after; a lane reading the pre-guard table would have concluded a distribution is worse than a coin flip. (c) SCREEN side (foundry.tiers.partition_corpus, S82 game-first-date iso_week basis, seed 0); nothing is FIT on the scored rows so there is no train fold to purge and the leak contract IS the strictly-before as-of guard, stated rather than dressed up as a walk-forward. (d) CROSS-MARKET CONSISTENCY: holding the as-of team SPLIT fixed and solving the rest-of-game scoring VOLUME L that reproduces the market's own moneyline (40-point log grid over [0.01,30]), then reading that distribution's total probability off against the market's own total price -- the only identifying restriction that is non-degenerate, since two free Poisson parameters against two market constraints are consistent by construction and holding the volume fixed instead makes the answer independent of the moneyline. Mean abs inconsistency mlb 0.2614 (inn 1-3 0.3339, inn 4-6 0.2421, inn 7+ 0.1861, n=25,998 at subsample step 3) and soccer 0.2699 (min 0-30 0.3469, 31-60 0.2865, 61+ 0.1753, n=3,279 unsubsampled); the moneyline was reproducible to within 0.02 by some L on 79.5 pct of mlb and 90.8 pct of soccer ticks, the rest tied or near-decided states counted in the mean rather than dropped. Both sports converge as the game resolves. THIS IS A JOINT STATEMENT ABOUT THE MARKET AND THE FIXED AS-OF SPLIT, NOT A PURE MARKET DEFECT: 0.26 in probability units is far too large to lay on the market, and the likeliest reading is that a season-to-date split with no starter, park, lineup or in-game information is the wrong shape -- which is what the Brier table says too. 2026-09-03 | signals-pregame | S108 | FIRST FULL-FEATURE REGULARISED PREGAME MODEL EVER SCREENED AGAINST THE INCUMBENT: NULL IN ALL FOUR SPORTS, 0 OF 8 ARMS CLEAR +0.004, AND THE NESTED INNER CV CHOSE ZERO FEATURES IN 20 OF 23 OUTER FOLDS. Premise HELD (Q8): grep over eval_gate + foundry returns combo_search.py (an NBA elastic net anchored on a real close) but it never calls tiers.partition_corpus, is NBA-only, picks its lambda from the OOF Briers themselves, and has NEVER been executed -- its canonical ledger data/cache/eval_gate/combo_fwer.json does not exist; s94/s96 use LogisticRegression(C=1e6) as an unpenalised in-game recalibration null. Rows = screen_predictor.corpus_states -> tiers.partition_corpus(seed=20260903) SCREEN side only, screen shas byte-equal to S58c/S79 (nba 1a32541d44aa7fcb, mlb ad743c924c7c4547, soccer 5c8d63970b08ce97, tennis c8dde4f3a44c8e58); screen n 867 / 19,589 / 7,656 / 17,352, scored n 619 / 16,791 / 6,562 / 14,873 over 5 / 6 / 6 / 6 expanding outer folds. Features = every numeric as-of column the gate corpus and the domain asof_*.parquet supply, each name-guarded through screen_predictor.check_feature_name: 103 + 75 missing indicators = 178 (nba), 11 + 11 = 22 (mlb), 29 + 25 = 54 (soccer), 90 + 88 = 178 (tennis); 85 refusals in total and EVERY ONE is 'unavailable' (a prior-count name carrying no asof marker, a >1-row-per-event source, or zero overlap), never 'leaky'; p_base/p_elo/p_home_elo dropped on nba/mlb after measuring them byte-identical to the offset. Two arms, both with logit(incumbent) as a TRUE OFFSET (coefficient fixed at 1): (a) elastic-net logistic through an own FISTA proximal solver (lam in 0.001..0.3, l1_ratio 0.5) written because sklearn cannot take an offset and statsmodels differs 0.14.4 local / 0.15.0 pod; (b) HistGradientBoostingRegressor on the Newton working response (y-p0)/(p0(1-p0)) with weights p0(1-p0), a glmboost-style single Newton step from the offset -- stated as the honest sklearn limit, since HistGradientBoostingClassifier has no init_score and lightgbm is NOT installed on the pod. Nested walk-forward, 3 inner expanding folds pick the penalty inside each outer train window, purge+embargo as one blanket 2-day date gap (STRICTER than the row's 1 day and than the harness 48 h purge), standardisation and median imputation inside the train fold only. INCUMBENT LABELLED: soccer/tennis = devigged close; nba/mlb = p_base = Elo, NOT a close (S98). RESULTS (improvement = Brier incumbent - Brier model): nba elastic_net 0.202605 -> 0.201245 = +0.001360, unit CI [-0.022932,+0.025651] (G=2), declared-key CI [-0.003367,+0.006086] (G=30, p 0.5608), PBO 0.000; nba hgb -0.002241; mlb elastic_net 0.243596 -> 0.243535 = +0.000061, unit CI [+0.000013,+0.000108] (G=2) but declared-key CI [-0.000121,+0.000243] (G=32, p 0.5015) and the improvement is 1/65 of the bar; mlb hgb -0.000567 (declared CI [-0.000923,-0.000211], p 0.0028 -- WORSE), PBO 0.645; soccer elastic_net 0.239657 -> 0.239690 = -0.000033; soccer hgb -0.001837; tennis elastic_net 0.196062 -> 0.196120 = -0.000058; tennis hgb -0.000865 (declared CI [-0.001320,-0.000410], p 0.0002 -- WORSE), PBO 0.623. Against a REAL devigged close all four arms are NEGATIVE; the best number anywhere is +0.001360, a third of the bar, on 619 events, against Elo. THE ACTUAL FINDING: in 20 of 23 outer folds the inner CV selected a penalty that zeroes EVERY coefficient, so the elastic-net arm collapsed to sigmoid(logit(p_incumbent) + a) -- an intercept-only recalibration. Verified from the archived per-event CSVs alone: sd(logit(p_enet) - logit(p_incumbent)) is EXACTLY 0.0 and Spearman(p_enet, p_incumbent) is 1.000000 in all 5 nba folds, all 6 tennis folds, 5/6 soccer and 4/6 mlb. So nba's +0.001360 is a base-rate recalibration of Elo, not a feature model; the same shift is worth -0.000033 / -0.000058 against a close, which needs none. Non-zero solutions ARE reachable (mlb folds 4-5 at lam 0.01 with 3 and 6 non-zero, soccer fold 2 at lam 0.03 with 2), so this is a selection result, not a solver artifact. HGB chose its most conservative config (depth 2, l2 100) in 20 of 23 folds. POD: deployed at md5 parity 3/3 from 55f71c000 (8aa8e8cf9dcc8d6aad0671691ff25f21, 26196ed40198aead6ef0eacf6f600d67, 7055ef09ded580c6539b0207a31d599c), no data shipped (S75/S78 corpora and asof tables already present; the one local/pod difference, data/domains/mlb/asof_espn_box.parquet, has 40 rows and zero corpus overlap so both feature sets are identical), FOUNDRY_PORTABLE_CORPUS=1, nohup + polled to the summary line, all four sports in under 3 min wall (23:42:49 -> 23:44:20 pod clock) on python 3.12 / pandas 3.0.5 / numpy 2.1.2 / sklearn 1.8.0; the nba arm reproduces the local run to the printed precision. 2026-09-03 | signals-pregame | S81 | THE OPEN-TO-CLOSE MOVE IS MEASURABLE ON TWO CORPORA AND IS NULL ON BOTH; THE SOCCER ARM IS CLOSED AT LIMIT ON THE BAR ITSELF. Premise (Q8): of nine local price corpora only two carry a pre-game OPEN beside a close -- data/domains/soccer/odds.parquet (ou_open_over/under vs ou_close_over/under, Pinnacle, 16,320 of 16,322 events, the same over/under-2.5 market the gate corpus labels) and data/cache/inplay_odds/mlb_price_series.parquet on Kalshi only (935 events). The row's nba clause is FALSIFIED: nba_checkpoints_full.parquet has ZERO rows with period <= 0 and its earliest tick per game sits at median game_clock_s 699 of period 1, ~21 s AFTER tip, so "first traded price = an open-ish" is an in-game price. tennis/odds.parquet has no open column at all (b365w/l, psw/l, maxw/l, avgw/l, ps_p1/p2, b365_p1/p2 are all CLOSING prices from different books); nba/tennis/soccer price series tickers (KXNBAGAME-26APR26BOSPHI, KXATPMATCH-26JUL01AUGPRI, KXEPLGAME-26MAY02AVLTOT, nba-ind-bos-2023-02-23) carry a DATE and no clock so no tick is certifiable pre-start; venue_history/nba_close_corpus.parquet is 663 rows of ONE close per game (validation_only); the mlb pitch tables carry no price column; data/cache/pm_paper does not exist. MLB RULE: the Kalshi ticker KXMLBGAME-<yy><MON><dd><hhmm><away><home> is the only local first-pitch clock (close_join_mlb already certifies it), open = FIRST two-sided TRADED quote strictly before first pitch, close = LAST such quote, both devigged through the existing close_column; median gap 71.15 h (p5-p95 64.9-71.7). `traded` is load-bearing (B9): on the first tick regardless of traded, 87.1 pct of opens sit at exactly 0.500 (the placeholder listing quote), which makes logit(open) constant and collapses the move back into S108's target -- the lane's first run produced R^2 0.673 that way and it was a degenerate denominator, not a result; on traded ticks only 3.2 pct sit at 0.500. MOVE DISTRIBUTION: soccer n 16,320 mean -0.0079 sd 0.1185 (p1..p99 -0.300..+0.282), corr(move, logit(open)) +0.071, walk-forward AR(1) R^2 +0.0033 -- the soccer open is already a real price. mlb n 913 mean +0.0902 sd 0.6894, corr -0.843, AR(1) R^2 +0.6101. BUILD: scripts/platformkit/eval_gate/s81_market_move.py (292 LOC) + tests/platformkit/eval_gate/test_s81_market_move.py (6 passed, 3.68 s); target logit(close)-logit(open) on the SCREEN side only (screen shas byte-equal to S58c/S79/S85/S108: soccer 5c8d63970b08ce97, mlb ad743c924c7c4547), features = every S108 as-of column name-guarded by screen_predictor.check_feature_name (imported, never edited -- S85 owns foundry/) PLUS logit_open, plus logit_p_base for mlb where the incumbent is Elo; p_close is never a feature; elastic-net LINEAR with the penalty chosen by an INNER expanding walk-forward, purge+embargo via S108's date gap (folds/_prep imported unchanged). RESULTS: soccer n 6,562 scored over 6 folds, 55 columns, enet R^2 vs the zero-move null +0.00412, sign accuracy 0.5393, AR(1) R^2 +0.00329; Brier open 0.240968 -> move-adjusted 0.240944 = +0.000024 CI [-0.000058,+0.000105]. mlb n 257 scored over 4 folds and 33 days (2026-06-02..2026-07-05), 24 columns, enet R^2 +0.85170 sign 0.7071, AR(1) R^2 +0.61008; Brier open 0.256005 -> adjusted 0.247455 = +0.008550 CI [-0.007206,+0.024306] (binding CI is the DECLARED team key, 25 clusters, because the mlb corpus is ONE corpus_unit). THE LOAD-BEARING FINDING is the ceiling, not the miss: close - open is +0.001311 Brier on soccer and +0.014658 on mlb, so on soccer totals a model with PERFECT foresight of the entire open-to-close move would still miss the frozen +0.004 bar by a factor of three -- the bar is unreachable by construction there and the arm is CLOSED AT LIMIT, the bar NOT lowered (Q3). SECOND FINDING: the mlb AR(1) coefficient is 0.987-1.021 in every fold, i.e. the fitted best estimate of the close is the base rate with the open DISCARDED; combined with corr -0.843, a single traded Kalshi tick three days before first pitch carries essentially no information about the pre-first-pitch close, so the +0.008550 is recovering a stale quote, not information the market lacked. Soccer's inner walk-forward drove every coefficient to zero in 3 of 6 outer folds, reproducing S108's behaviour against a different target. NO PREREG DRAFT: the draft bar is >= +0.004 with the CI excluding zero; soccer misses by 170x with the CI straddling zero, mlb clears the point estimate but its CI straddles zero and its window is SINGLE-WINDOW (33 days, one venue, one corpus_unit) so it could not carry an AHEAD regardless. Uncharged: no seal, K never read, _charge_ledger never called, backtest_fwer.jsonl never opened (18 rows, md5 a4ae7c13995672e478d59770591b83ba), data/registry untouched, no flag flipped, verdict partition never built or read, nothing read or written under src/kernel/api/intel/team_system/foundry. Premise (Q8) HOLDS: the S86 screen CSV is on disk (232,951 ticks x 23 cols, 797 games, 2024-10-22..2026-06-10) and a repo-wide grep of scripts/platformkit/eval_gate for HistGradientBoosting|MLPClassifier|MLPRegressor returns exactly one module, s108_pregame_full_model.py, which is PREGAME and never opens the tick CSV -- the three tick-CSV readers (s94, s97, s98) import no non-linear learner, so no non-linear arm with the market as an offset existed (0/3). BUILD: scripts/platformkit/eval_gate/s115_ingame_models.py (299 LOC) + tests/platformkit/ingame/test_s115_ingame_models.py (5 passed in 4.50 s). p = sigmoid(logit(market) + f(state)) with logit(market) a TRUE offset (coefficient fixed at 1, never fit), f by ONE Newton step on the logistic working response (the S108 trick); 8 features = period, clock_frac, margin, dmargin over the past 3/5/10 ticks of the same game, logit(market), logit(prior); the lag builder past_delta() raises ValueError('leak guard') if the borrowed row's ts is not strictly earlier, so a same-tick or later read cannot degrade silently. Arms: hgb (HistGradientBoostingRegressor max_depth 3, sample_weight = w, l2 grid 10/100), mlp (MLPRegressor (16,), early stopping on a TRAIN-internal 15 pct split, alpha grid 1e-3/1e-1), hgb_mono (the hgb arm with monotonic_cst = margin increasing). NULL = the S94 global recalibration LogisticRegression on [1, logit(market)] fit on the IDENTICAL train rows (s94._recal imported, not copied). Design imported from S94 (fold_dates): expanding walk-forward by game-first date, purge by game (asserted disjoint), 1-day embargo, 5 outer folds, 2 inner folds pick each config on train only; _prep imported from S108. Two documented deviations, neither touching a bar: MLPClassifier cannot regress a continuous working response so MLPRegressor is used (and, having no sample_weight, minimises unweighted MSE on a working response with w floored at 5e-2 vs the HGB arms' S108 floor of 1e-3); and with logit(market) as the offset a zero-capacity arm reproduces the RAW MARKET (< 1e-12, tested) rather than the recal null, so both facts are tested separately. RESULT on 192,635 held-out ticks / 673 games over 5 folds: raw market Brier 0.078611, recal null 0.078974, hgb 0.080022 (vs market -0.001411, game-clustered CI [-0.002918,+0.000096], p 0.066), mlp 0.079160 (-0.000549, CI [-0.001476,+0.000378], p 0.245), hgb_mono 0.080066 (-0.001455, CI [-0.002982,+0.000073], p 0.062). Best arm mlp; headline -0.000549 against a +0.004 bar. All three are also behind the recal null (-0.000185 / -0.001048 / -0.001091). ICC by game 0.2050, design effect 59.5, n_eff 3,239.8; n_informative 78,761 of 192,635 and the informative re-quote is the same side of zero (-0.001238, CI [-0.003156,+0.000680], p 0.205). PBO 0.071 across the 6-config grid. SECOND FINDING: both HGB arms picked the STRONGEST l2 in all 5 of 5 folds -- the inner CV asked for the least capacity the grid offered, the same shape S108 and S111 reported when their inner walk-forward drove every coefficient to zero. AS-OF GUARD TESTED: appending a substitution AFTER a tick leaves that tick's feature byte-identical, a same-tick sub is not applied, AsOfViolation raised, and unit_history snapshots a date's targets BEFORE any game of that date is booked.
